MEMORANDUM 
TO: 
 
Mayor Patrick Collins, and City Council 
FROM: 
 
TJ Barttelbort, Purchasing Manager 
DATE: 
 
April 6, 2021  
SUBJECT: 
Consideration of Bid #E-19-21 for furnishing Concealable Body Armor, for the 
City of Cheyenne, Cheyenne Police Department 
______________________________________________ 
Two (2) bidders responded to this bid, and the bid tabulation is attached.  The two (2) bids were 
received from: 
 
1. Galls LLC.  Lexington, KY. 
2. Skaggs Public Safety.  Centennial, CO. 
 
The bids were reviewed by Lew Simpson (CPD Equipment Quartermaster).  His recommendation 
is to accept the lowest responsive bid from Galls LLC.   
 
Bid E-19-21 was solicited to provide Concealable Body Armor for a three (3) year period.  Galls 
LLC provide the lowest bid price for all three (3) years, and did not provide any price increases 
across the three (3) year term of this Bid Award.   
 
Due to the nature of how the Concealable Body Armor is issued to officers and new hires, exact 
quantities needed for a three (3) year period are not precisely known at this time.  Based on 
calculated estimates, the Cheyenne Police Department is requesting approval for $79,610.00 for a 
three (3) year period (Estimated Quantities Required, included for reference).   
 
If unforeseen changes arise, additional purchases may be required.  Any additional unforeseen 
purchases will be made in accordance with the individual line item pricing, submitted by Galls 
LLC, in accordance with the Bidding Documents.

(024-15-1530-41-85409 / Bulletproof Vest Program Grant) 
(001-15-1511-41-21330 / Small Grant Match)

# SOURCE_FILE: CHEY-2021-04-06_TotalExtraction_75644531.pdf
 
 
MEMORANDUM 
TO: 
 
Mayor Patrick Collins, and City Council 
FROM: 
 
TJ Barttelbort, Purchasing Manager 
DATE: 
 
April 5, 2021  
SUBJECT: 
Consideration of Bid #E-20-21 for a Contract between the City of Cheyenne and 
Alco Fence Co. Inc., for the Fencing and Gates, for the City of Cheyenne Compost 
Facility Project 
______________________________________________ 
Three (3) bidders responded to this bid, and the bid tabulation is attached.  The Three (3) bids were 
received from: 
 
1. SWi LLC.  Cheyenne, Wyoming. 
a. Bid Rejected, Not In Compliance 
2. Borderline Fencing, Inc.  Cheyenne, Wyoming.  
a. Bid Rejected, Not In Compliance  
3. Alco Fence Co. Inc.  Cheyenne, Wyoming. 
a. Bid submitted In Compliance 
 
Two (2) of the Three (3) Bids were not in compliance with the terms of the Bidding Documents, 
and the non-compliant bids have been rejected.  One (1) Submitted Bid was fully in compliance. 
 
The basis for Bid Rejection, was based on the Bidders not returning their “Marked” Line Item 
Specifications / Scope of Work.  The Two (2) Bids that have been rejected did not mark Y or N, 
on ANY of the Line Items.  The requirement to return the “Marked” Line Item Specifications / 
Scope of Work, for review by the City, is clearly identified in two (2) separate locations within the 
Bidding Documents.  Due to the fact that the Bidders did not indicate Y or N, we have no 
mechanism to certify that their submitted Bids, and their end product, will meet our Specification 
/ Scope of Work. 
 
The bids were reviewed by Vicki Nemecek (Public Works), Craig LaVoy (Public Works), and 
Matt Theriault (Public Works).  Their recommendation is to accept the lowest responsive bid from 
Alco Fence Co. Inc.  The bid award is for $47,740.00. 
 
Funding:  
023-14-1423-43-43102 (Solid Waste Fund / Equipment (over $5,000))

CITY OF CHEYENNE, WYOMING 
AGREEMENT 
 
Bid No. E-20-21 
 
Contract No. _______ 
 
THIS AGREEMENT, entered into this ____ day of ___________, _____, by and between 
the CITY OF CHEYENNE, WYOMING, hereinafter referred to as the “CITY”, and ALCO 
FENCE CO. INC., hereinafter referred to as the “CONTRACTOR”. 
 
WITNESSETH that the Contractor and the City, for the considerations stated herein, 
mutually agree as follows: 
 
ARTICLE 1.  STATEMENT OF WORK.  The Contractor shall furnish all supervision, 
technical personnel, labor, materials, machinery, tools, equipment, and services, including utility 
and transportation services, and perform and complete all work in an efficient and workmanlike 
manner in the construction of the “Compost Facility Fencing & Gates” project all in strict 
accordance with the Contract Documents including all addenda thereto, numbered and dated: 
Addendum One (1), dated March 9, 2021. 
 
ARTICLE 2.  RESPONSIBLE DESIGNEE FOR THE CITY.  The Contractor shall in any 
and all matters relating to the scope of services to be provided under this Contract or any other 
provisions herein, contact the City Engineer, or his/her designated representative. 
 
ARTICLE 3.  TIME FRAME FOR COMPLETION.  The services to be performed under 
this Agreement shall commence on the date stipulated in the “Notice to Proceed” that will be issued 
by the City.  The work shall be completed by July 2, 2021.  If the work has not been completed 
within the time stipulated above, including any extensions of time issued by the City for excusable 
delays, the Contractor and his/her sureties shall pay the City fixed, agreed liquidated damages, as 
stipulated in the Bidding Documents, for each calendar day of delay until the work is completed. 
 
ARTICLE 4.  COMPENSATION AND METHOD OF PAYMENT.  The CITY will pay 
the CONTRACTOR for the performance of the Contract in current funds, the sum of Forty-Seven 
Thousand, Seven Hundred Forty Dollars ($47,740.00).  In the event there are changes in the 
estimated quantities shown on the Bid Proposal, the unit prices multiplied by the actual quantities 
shall govern, and the total contract amount will be adjusted accordingly.  The City agrees to pay 
the above amount for contractual services in the following manner, upon receipt of appropriate 
documentation: 
 
a. 
The Contractor will be paid on a monthly basis for percentage of estimated work completed.  
Submittal will be at least seven (7) business days prior to the payable due date as established 
annually by the City Treasurer’s Office.  The pay request shall be submitted on the Contract 
Payment Request Form and Itemized Pay Request or the AIA Documents G702 and G703.  
The engineer will review the estimate for approval prior to payment. 
 
By Alessandra at 11:30 am, Apr 02, 2021

b. 
The City will withhold five percent (5%) of the dollar value of the work completed for a 
minimum of forty-one (41) calendar days after Notice of Final Settlement has been published 
in accordance with Wyo. Stat. §15-1-113(h).  Upon completion of the work under this 
Contract, the Contractor shall submit a Contractor’s Certificate of Completion; Final Waivers 
of Lien from the Contractor, and all Sub-Contractors, Suppliers and Materialmen; Affidavit 
of Release of Liens; Affidavit of Payment; and a current Workers Compensation Certificate.  
Final payment will not be made until the above documents have been received by the City 
and all items on the Punch List have been completed, and the advertising requirements have 
been met. 
 
ARTICLE 5.  CONTRACT.  The executed Contract Documents shall consist of the 
following: 
 
1. This Agreement; 
2. Addenda; 
3. Invitation for Bids; 
4. Instructions to Bidders; 
5. Signed Bid Proposal; 
6. General Provisions and Insurance; 
7. Specifications and Special 
Provisions; and  
8. Drawings. 
 
This Agreement, together with other documents enumerated in this Article 5, which said other 
documents are as fully a part of the Contract as if hereto attached or herein repeated, forms the 
Contract between the parties hereto. 
 
IN WITNESS WHEREOF, THAT the governing body of the City of Cheyenne has authorized 
the Mayor as Executive Officer of the City to enter into this Agreement, and that the parties 
hereto have caused this Agreement to be executed on the day and year in the first part herein 
written.

CITY OF CHEYENNE, WYOMING 
AGREEMENT 
 
Bid No. S-16-21 
 
Contract No. _______ 
 
THIS AGREEMENT, entered into this ____ day of ___________, _____, by and between 
the CITY OF CHEYENNE, WYOMING, hereinafter referred to as the “CITY”, and CRAIG 
CONSTRUCTION SERVICES LLC, hereinafter referred to as the “CONTRACTOR”. 
 
WITNESSETH that the Contractor and the City, for the considerations stated herein, 
mutually agree as follows: 
 
ARTICLE 1.  STATEMENT OF WORK.  The Contractor shall furnish all supervision, 
technical personnel, labor, materials, machinery, tools, equipment, and services, including utility 
and transportation services, and perform and complete all work in an efficient and workmanlike 
manner in the construction of the “Cheyenne Aquatics Center Main Pool Diving Boards 
Removal & Replacement” project all in strict accordance with the Contract Documents including 
all addenda thereto, numbered and dated: Addendum One (1), dated March 18, 2021, Addendum 
Two (2), dated March 25, 2021, and Addendum Three (3), dated March 29, 2021. 
 
ARTICLE 2.  RESPONSIBLE DESIGNEE FOR THE CITY.  The Contractor shall in any 
and all matters relating to the scope of services to be provided under this Contract or any other 
provisions herein, contact the City Engineer, or his/her designated representative. 
 
ARTICLE 3.  TIME FRAME FOR COMPLETION.  The services to be performed under 
this Agreement shall commence on the date stipulated in the “Notice to Proceed” that will be issued 
by the City.  The work shall be completed by August 31, 2021.  If the work has not been completed 
within the time stipulated above, including any extensions of time issued by the City for excusable 
delays, the Contractor and his/her sureties shall pay the City fixed, agreed liquidated damages, as 
stipulated in the Supplemental Conditions, for each calendar day of delay until the work is 
completed. 
 
ARTICLE 4.  COMPENSATION AND METHOD OF PAYMENT.  The CITY will pay 
the CONTRACTOR for the performance of the Contract in current funds, the sum of Sixty-Two 
Thousand, Two Hundred Thirty Dollars ($62,230.00).  In the event there are changes in the 
estimated quantities shown on the Bid Proposal, the unit prices multiplied by the actual quantities 
shall govern, and the total contract amount will be adjusted accordingly.  The City agrees to pay 
the above amount for contractual services in the following manner, upon receipt of appropriate 
documentation: 
 
a. 
The Contractor will be paid on a monthly basis for percentage of estimated work completed.  
Submittal will be at least seven (7) business days prior to the payable due date as established 
annually by the City Treasurer’s Office.  The pay request shall be submitted on the Contract 
Payment Request Form and Itemized Pay Request or the AIA Documents G702 and G703.  
The engineer will review the estimate for approval prior to payment. 
By Michael R. O'Donnell at 9:23 am, Apr 19, 2021

b. 
The City will withhold five percent (5%) of the dollar value of the work completed for a 
minimum of forty-one (41) calendar days after Notice of Final Settlement has been published 
in accordance with Wyo. Stat. §15-1-113(h).  Upon completion of the work under this 
Contract, the Contractor shall submit a Contractor’s Certificate of Completion; the Consent 
of Surety; Final Waivers of Lien from the Contractor, and all Sub-Contractors, Suppliers and 
Materialmen; Affidavit of Release of Liens; Affidavit of Payment; and a current Workers 
Compensation Certificate.  Final payment will not be made until the above documents have 
been received by the City and all items on the Punch List have been completed, and the 
advertising requirements have been met. 
 
ARTICLE 5.  CONTRACT.  The executed Contract Documents shall consist of the 
following: 
 
1. This Agreement; 
2. Addenda; 
3. Invitation for Bids; 
4. Instructions to Bidders; 
5. Signed Bid Proposal; 
 
6. General Conditions and Insurance; 
7. Supplemental Conditions; 
8. Part IV Forms & Notices; 
9. Specifications and Special 
Provisions; and  
10. Drawings. 
 
This Agreement, together with other documents enumerated in this Article 5, which said other 
documents are as fully a part of the Contract as if hereto attached or herein repeated, forms the 
Contract between the parties hereto. 
 
IN WITNESS WHEREOF, THAT the governing body of the City of Cheyenne has authorized 
the Mayor as Executive Officer of the City to enter into this Agreement, and that the parties 
hereto have caused this Agreement to be executed on the day and year in the first part herein 
written. 
 
ATTEST:
